- **lc-ms-batch-effect-quantification**: Use when LC-MS metabolomics data contain identical biological samples run in subsequent batches (between-batch replicate pairs) and you need to quantify which normalization restores cross-batch intensity correspondence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-batch-effect-quantification))
- **lc-ms-batch-inspection**: Use when a batch of raw LC-MS (or LC-IMS-MS, DDA/DIA, infusion) files needs a fast, omics-agnostic global quality assessment to spot outlier runs or instrument drift prior to targeted peak-metric analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-batch-inspection))
- **lc-ms-candidate-scoring-formula-implementation**: Use when re-implementing or verifying how MetaboAnnotatoR computes per-feature match scores and ranks candidate metabolites from AIF pseudo-MS/MS fragment matches and m/z error, or when cross-checking rankScore/searchLib outputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-candidate-scoring-formula-implementation))
- **lc-ms-chromatographic-peak-detection**: Use when raw centroid-mode LC–MS mzML files need chromatographic peak picking and feature detection prior to feature table generation in untargeted metabolomics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-chromatographic-peak-detection))
- **lc-ms-classification-model-training**: Use when raw, high-dimensional MS vectors (e.g., binned to 12000 m/z features, TIC-normalized) must be classified end-to-end with a learnable representation module and compared against baselines via cross-validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-classification-model-training))
- **lc-ms-concentration-result-correlation-analysis**: Use when a targeted LC-MS/MS quantification experiment has been processed with multiple drift-correction/quantification strategies and you must quantify their agreement (e.g. test whether non-IS strategies correlate at Pearson r &gt; 0.9). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-concentration-result-correlation-analysis))
- **lc-ms-cross-dataset-feature-matching**: Use when two separately peak-picked LC–MS metabolomics feature tables (samples × features with RT, m/z, FI) must be combined or compared — e.g., batch combination, discovery validation, or multicohort integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-cross-dataset-feature-matching))
- **lc-ms-data-calibration**: Use when when you have paired LC-MS measurements from labeled and unlabeled samples of the same analytes, and you need to isolate the true isotopic labeling contribution by removing confounding signal from naturally occurring isotopes and tracer isotopic impurity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-data-calibration) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-data-ingestion-preprocessing**: Use when starting a Nextflow4MS-DIAL (MS-DIAL/MS-FLO) run with vendor-proprietary LC-HRMS RAW files that must first be converted to .mzML or .abf before the pipeline's MS-DIAL processing step. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-data-ingestion-preprocessing))
- **lc-ms-data-pipeline-architecture**: Use when you have vendor-format LC-MS acquisition files (.raw, .d, .ms) from instrument runs and need to set up an end-to-end data quality control system that converts proprietary formats into open mzML, processes spectral data, and surfaces QC failures in real time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-data-pipeline-architecture) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-data-preprocessing**: Use when you have raw mzML files and corresponding feature tables (CSV format, mzmine-formatted) from untargeted LCMS experiments, and you need to convert them into uniformly-shaped peak matrices (2 × 120 per peak: margin + signal regions) as input for neural network classification of MS1 peak. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-data-preprocessing) · 3 copies)
- **lc-ms-data-structure-validation**: Use when before launching TARDIS peak detection on a new LC–MS dataset or target compound list. Apply this skill when you have raw MS data files in vendor formats (e.g., .raw, .d) and/or a spreadsheet-based target list (.xlsx or . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-data-structure-validation) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-dataset-acquisition-and-curation**: Use when when beginning an untargeted LC-MS metabolomics study and need to assemble a cohort of mzML files for processing; particularly when establishing performance baselines across sample counts (10, 50, 100+ samples), validating reproducibility, or preparing data for publication. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-dataset-acquisition-and-curation) · 4 copies)
- **lc-ms-dataset-pair-alignment**: Use when two peak-picked, conventionally aligned untargeted LC-MS data sets were acquired under different conditions (e.g. different gradient elution) and you need to determine overlapping features and concatenate abundances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-dataset-pair-alignment))
- **lc-ms-drift-correction-bracketing**: Use when targeted LC-MS/MS peak areas (e.g. exported from MultiQuant 3.0.3) show intensity drift across injection order and internal standards are unavailable or unsuitable, requiring QC-based drift correction or calibration bracketing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-drift-correction-bracketing))
- **lc-ms-drift-residual-correlation-testing**: Use when a targeted LC-MS/MS sequence has been quantified under one or more drift correction strategies (IS correction, QC-based drift correction, custom bracketing, weighted bracketing) and you need to test whether injection-order-dependent bias remains. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-drift-residual-correlation-testing))
- **lc-ms-drift-simulation-data-generation**: Use when you must benchmark QC-based LC-MS normalization methods (QC-RLSC, rLOESS, rGAM, tGAM) on simulated data with known drift ground truth and injected outliers, scoring median squared prediction error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-drift-simulation-data-generation))
- **lc-ms-eic-peak-detection**: Use when processing mzML files of authentic metabolite standards to detect and quality-filter peaks per adduct EIC before assigning retention times and building an RT/m/z reference library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-eic-peak-detection))
- **lc-ms-eic-plot-interpretation**: Use when after executing TARDIS in screening_mode = TRUE on centroided .mzML files with a defined target compound list. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-eic-plot-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-feature-alignment-algorithm-implementation**: Use when multiple LC-MS run feature tables (CSV with m/z, RT, area columns) must be aligned into consensus features beyond naive nearest-neighbor matching, i.e. all potential cross-run feature correspondences must be considered jointly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-alignment-algorithm-implementation))
- **lc-ms-feature-alignment-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantify how an RT alignment tool degrades as retention-time shift variability increases, using simulated feature lists with known ground-truth correspondences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-alignment-benchmarking))
- **lc-ms-feature-alignment-cross-dataset**: Use when you have two peak-picked, conventionally aligned LC-MS metabolomics datasets (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-feature-alignment-cross-dataset) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-feature-alignment-evaluation**: Use when you need to quantitatively evaluate how accurately a feature alignment method (e.g. G-Aligner, MZmine2, OpenMS, XCMS) aligns consensus features across multiple LC-MS runs against a manually annotated reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-alignment-evaluation))
- **lc-ms-feature-annotation-pipeline-execution**: Use when a feature peak table from LC-MS untargeted metabolomics must be annotated against KEGG and per-peak compound candidates need diffusion-based prioritization rather than plain m/z matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-annotation-pipeline-execution))
- **lc-ms-feature-based-nta-workflow-execution**: Use when raw LC-HRMS data (e.g. standard/blank triplicates in positive and negative ionization) must be turned into annotated features and linked parent–transformation-product components using an R-based NTA workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-based-nta-workflow-execution))
- **lc-ms-feature-correspondence-mapping**: Use when aligning feature lists from Dinosaur, OpenMS, MaxQuant, XICFinder, or TXT/CSV (m/z, charge, RT, intensity) across a large cohort of LC-MS runs where RT shifts are present and existing match-between-runs or warping tools fail. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-correspondence-mapping))
- **lc-ms-feature-correspondence-matching**: Use when integrating two LC-MS metabolomics datasets (e.g., combining batches, multicohort studies) and only feature RT, m/z, and FI (no annotation) are available to find which features correspond between the datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-correspondence-matching))
- **lc-ms-feature-detection**: Use when centroided mzML LC-MS/MS runs (e.g. QTOF or Orbitrap spatial-mapping datasets) must be converted into a feature quantification matrix as the entry point of an Optimus/'ili cartography analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-detection--d85aea3833c85468))
- **lc-ms-feature-detection**: Use when raw centroided LC-MS1 spectra (e.g. mzML files from Metabolomics Workbench accessions like ST002796/ST002798) must be converted into a quantified MS1 feature table as the precursor step to MS2 deconvolution, DB searching, or pathway analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-detection--512a653b5701a1cd))
- **lc-ms-feature-detection**: Use when starting from raw LC-MS files (mzML or Bruker timsTOF .d/TDF2 DDA-PASEF) and you need nontargeted peak detection with 100% signal coverage, edge-bounded peak segments, and feature quality metadata. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-detection--59ec06857f03beaf))
- **lc-ms-feature-detection**: Use when raw UHPLC-HRMS2 DDA data (Thermo RAW converted to mzXML/mzML) must be converted into a per-sample feature quantification table plus per-feature MS2 spectra (.mgf), before structural annotation or FBMN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-detection--e300bd20a9eef616))
- **lc-ms-feature-detection**: Use when starting from centroided mzML (or Thermo .raw) acquisitions and an experiment directory with sample-name-to-file-path metadata, and you need feature tables (m/z + retention time per feature) for downstream QC, normalization, or annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-detection--c9e2ccb47b831ded))
- **lc-ms-feature-detection**: Use when you have triplicate LC-HRMS data (e.g. standard-X/solvent-X) acquired in both positive and negative ionization modes and need a single aligned feature table spanning both polarities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-detection--361fba201e66becd))
- **lc-ms-feature-detection-and-alignment**: Use when raw LC-MS metabolomics data (e.g. MTBLS146 maternal plasma files in ~/GalaxyM-TestData/LCMS_DATA) must be converted from vendor spectra into a peak/feature table aligned across biological, blank and QC samples before filtering or PCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-detection-and-alignment))
- **lc-ms-feature-detection-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or comparing LC-MS metabolomics feature-detection tools on data with known ground truth, or when tuning FeatureFinderMetabo parameters under varying detector noise, mass error (e.g. 10 ppm vs 40 ppm), and distorted elution profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-detection-benchmarking--7606bd871679eb50))
- **lc-ms-feature-detection-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or comparing LC-MS metabolomics feature-detection tools against a manually verified/certified reference feature list, or when reproducing the asari vs XCMS/MZmine benchmark counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-detection-benchmarking--a9e78f411ab91e45))
- **lc-ms-feature-detection-centwave**: Use when starting from centroid-mode mzML raw LC–MS files and per-sample chromatographic/spectral peaks must be located and converted into (retention time, m/z) features for downstream feature matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-detection-centwave))
- **lc-ms-feature-extraction**: Use when processing raw mzXML LC-MS metabolomics or exposomics data (DDA mode) where low-abundant or bad-shape features are missed by conventional peak picking and deeper feature coverage is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-extraction--ff36c5f2df258e14))
- **lc-ms-feature-extraction**: Use when starting from publicly deposited LC–MS/MS mzML raw files (e.g. Metabolomics Workbench) and you need chromatographic features with linked representative MS2 spectra as a feature table for downstream annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-extraction--be3ca852ec598f5a))
- **lc-ms-feature-extraction-and-alignment**: Use when you have centroid mzML files from LC-MS experiments (converted from Thermo .raw or other vendor formats) and need to identify and quantify individual chemical features across multiple samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-feature-extraction-and-alignment) · 4 copies)
- **lc-ms-feature-extraction-openms**: Use when raw mzML LC-MS runs (e.g. MTBLS562, QE_HF, TripleTOF_6600 benchmark datasets) must be converted into per-run feature lists (m/z, RT, area) for downstream alignment or for reproducing an OpenMS-based baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-extraction-openms))
- **lc-ms-feature-filtering**: Use when a PATBS-processed LC-MS feature table with m/z, MS1/MS2, and retention information must be filtered to link in vivo metabolites to prototypes, especially when metabolites follow unpredictable (non-obvious) metabolic pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-filtering))
- **lc-ms-feature-finding**: Use when raw LC-HRMS data (e.g., a standard mixture vs. blank solvent, each in triplicate) must be converted to features grouped across analyses, especially to compare feature-finding algorithms or build a consensus feature list. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-finding))
- **lc-ms-feature-grouping-analysis**: Use when LC-MS peak data has been grouped by XCMS at two RT-window (bw) resolutions and you need to identify which peak groups (features) are misaligned before realignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-grouping-analysis))
- **lc-ms-feature-grouping-and-compounding**: Use when after chromatographic peak detection on preprocessed LC-MS data, when you have hundreds or thousands of individual m/z × retention-time peaks and need to associate them into biologically meaningful feature groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-feature-grouping-and-compounding) · 4 copies)
- **lc-ms-feature-grouping-by-retention-time**: Use when immediately after chromatographic peak detection (findChromPeaks) when you have detected peaks across multiple samples and need to identify which peaks represent the same feature across the sample cohort. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-feature-grouping-by-retention-time) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-feature-grouping-metacorrelate**: Use when processing aligned LC-MS/MS feature lists where redundant ion species of the same compound must be connected before uploading to GNPS feature-based molecular networking with IIMN enabled. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-grouping-metacorrelate))
- **lc-ms-feature-m-z-rt-extraction**: Use when you have preprocessed LC-MS intensity data (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-feature-m-z-rt-extraction) · 3 copies)
- **lc-ms-feature-matching-across-platforms**: Use when two or more LC-MS feature tables of the same or near-identical features were acquired on different mass spectrometry platforms and you need to test annotation robustness by comparing metabolite annotations against the same plant-specific MS database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-matching-across-platforms))
- **lc-ms-feature-matching-between-datasets**: Use when two LC–MS feature tables (XCMS peak-picked, post-QC, each with RT, m/z, and FI medians per feature) must be matched across batches or cohorts, and Step 1 candidate matching leaves features in clusters with multiple matches requiring unique correspondence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-matching-between-datasets))
- **lc-ms-feature-matrix-missing-value-imputation**: Use when a peak-intensity profile table from LC-MS or SRM-MS metabolomics/proteomics contains missing values that must be filled before applying the 80% rule-based peak filter, QC-RFSC drift correction, or multivariate statistics in statTarget. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-matrix-missing-value-imputation))
- **lc-ms-feature-mz-annotation**: Use when you have a pre-processed MS1 feature table (m/z + retention time) and a compound reference database with exact masses and RTs, and retention times are only partially transferable between labs or methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-mz-annotation))
- **lc-ms-feature-pair-classifier-training**: Use when labeled feature-pair data from aligned LC-MS runs (attributes: m/z, charge, RT, intensity) are available and the goal is to train or benchmark classifiers, e.g. testing whether a DNN outperforms RF/KNN/SVM/LR by AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-pair-classifier-training))
- **lc-ms-feature-quality-assessment**: Use when you have generated feature tables from LC-MS data using different parameter combinations (e.g., varying Centwave, FeatureFinderMetabo, or ADAP peak picking settings) and need to objectively compare their outputs to select the -performing configuration for your dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-feature-quality-assessment) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-feature-quality-filtering**: Use when raw mzML/mzXML LC-MS/MS files must be quality-filtered into feature tables prior to SIRIUS/ZODIAC formula annotation, or when reported per-dataset feature counts before and after filtering must be reproduced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-quality-filtering))
- **lc-ms-feature-quality-flagging**: Use when a preprocessed LC-MS peak table (e.g. MS-DIAL output) must be quality-controlled before statistics: features detected in &lt;70% of QC samples or with RSD ≥ 0.2 / D-ratio ≥ 0.4 need flagging. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-quality-flagging))
- **lc-ms-feature-quality-metric-computation**: Use when you have an XCMS peak-picked LC–MS untargeted metabolomics run (with pooled QC samples) and need numeric per-peak quality metrics to feed peak-quality classifiers or flag low-quality integrations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-quality-metric-computation))
- **lc-ms-feature-quality-scoring**: Use when immediately after peak detection and feature table generation from LC-MS data, when you need to rank or filter features by confidence before annotation or statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-feature-quality-scoring) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-feature-quantification**: Use when centroided LC/MS data (mzML/mzXML) from multiple biological groups must be converted into a feature table (m/z, retention time, group-wise intensities) for comparative metabolomics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-quantification))
- **lc-ms-feature-quantification-processing**: Use when you have mzML LC–MS/MS runs needing feature detection, quantification, filtering, and level-2 putative compound annotation, or when MS/MS spectra must be exported (MGF/MAT) for GNPS, Sirius, or MS-FINDER dereplication. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-quantification-processing))
- **lc-ms-feature-row-tabulation**: Use when comparing alignment algorithms (RANSAC vs Join) on LC-MS peak lists and row counts are needed to explain why one aligner degrades on high-feature-count fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-row-tabulation))
- **lc-ms-feature-statistical-testing**: Use when the input is a preprocessed MetaboSet object (or imputed feature abundance matrix with group labels) and the goal is per-feature group comparisons — selecting which molecular features differ between two or more study groups with multiplicity-corrected p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-statistical-testing))
- **lc-ms-feature-table-annotation**: Use when you have a feature/peak table with accurate m/z, RT, and intensities from a fixed LC-MS assay (e.g., HILIC- or CSH-Q Exactive HF) plus a same-condition in-house m/z+RT library, and MS/MS spectra are absent, sparse, or not required for level 2 annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-table-annotation))
- **lc-ms-feature-table-batch-correction**: Use when a PCPFM-derived feature table (TSV) exhibits batch-driven sub-clustering in QAQC PCA plots and sample metadata contains a batch designation field, and missing/uncommon features have already been removed or imputed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-table-batch-correction))
- **lc-ms-feature-table-cleaning**: Use when you have a processed LC-MS untargeted metabolomics feature table (features x samples) with missing values in QC and biological samples, and you need a cleaned, imputed, normalized intensity table before statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-table-cleaning))
- **lc-ms-feature-table-formatting-and-filtering**: Use when peak-picked LC-HRMS feature tables from two untargeted metabolomics data sets must be cleaned and formatted before feature pair alignment with metabCombiner. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-table-formatting-and-filtering))
- **lc-ms-feature-table-generation**: Use when raw LC–MS metabolomics data (netCDF/mzXML vendor files) must be converted into a sample-by-feature table via XCMS peak identification, RT correction, and grouping, prior to peak-quality labeling or MetaClean classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-table-generation))
- **lc-ms-feature-table-ingestion**: Use when starting an mpactR workflow with an exported peak table (e.g. Progenesis CSV with compound, m/z, retention time, and raw abundance columns) and a companion sample metadata CSV that must be loaded into a single R object before applying filters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-table-ingestion))
- **lc-ms-feature-table-processing**: Use when you have an LC-MS feature table (samples × metabolite features in negative ion mode) plus tissue/group labels, and need to test whether a specific tissue (e.g., buccal gland) separates from others in PCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-table-processing--0d2ed2115aafa5e3))
- **lc-ms-feature-table-processing**: Use when you have an Asari feature table (preferred_Feature_table.tsv or full_feature_table.tsv) from an LC-MS experiment and need QC curation or grouping of features into empirical compounds (empCpds) before MS1/MS2 annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-table-processing--751eebd24a34fae9))
- **lc-ms-feature-table-quality-filtering**: Use when a processed LC–MS peak/feature table still contains missing values and you must remove noisy features by MV thresholds before imputation, differential analysis, and pathway enrichment in the tidyMass framework. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-feature-table-quality-filtering))
- **lc-ms-gc-ms-data-preprocessing**: Use when raw LC-MS or GC-MS metabolite abundance matrices (metabolites x isolates, e.g. yogurt fermentations with 182 added starter strains) need normalization and feature filtering before dimensionality reduction or association testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/lc-ms-gc-ms-data-preprocessing))
- **lc-ms-gradient-encoding-vector-representation**: Use when when you have a set of candidate LC gradients (parameter combinations) that you wish to evaluate with a Gaussian process model, or when you need to convert raw gradient specifications into a standardized numerical format for Bayesian optimization acquisition function computation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-gradient-encoding-vector-representation) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-homologue-series-detection**: Use when given an LC-MS feature/peak table (mz, rt, intensity) and the goal is to group features into homologue series defined by repeating mass increments (e.g. 44.02628 Da C2H4O) with correlated retention-time steps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-homologue-series-detection))
- **lc-ms-intensity-matrix-reconstruction**: Use when an XCMS-derived peak intensity matrix (retention time, m/z, peak intensity) shows batch or injection-order drift (visible intensity drift over run order, dispersed QCSs) and the corrected intensity matrix must be rebuilt without batch labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-intensity-matrix-reconstruction))
- **lc-ms-intensity-noise-modeling**: Use when simulating synthetic LC-MS/MS peaks in mzML and noise standard deviation must scale with signal intensity (e.g. reproducing SMITER's noise model or validating a replacement injector). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-intensity-noise-modeling))
- **lc-ms-isotope-feature-grouping**: Use when raw LC-MS data (mzML or Bruker .d) has been feature-detected and aligned, and isotope/adduct/ISF peak groups must be annotated to remove false-positive features before statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-isotope-feature-grouping))
- **lc-ms-maintenance-event-alignment**: Use when you have time-ordered DIA and paired DDA QC runs from a monitored instrument with maintenance-event timestamps, and need to determine whether DIA-based QC metrics (e.g. MS2 intensity) respond more sensitively than DDA metrics (e.g. MS1 area) to system status changes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-maintenance-event-alignment))
- **lc-ms-mass-matching-reference-building**: Use when at the start of an untargeted LC-MS annotation pipeline when you have a KEGG database with exact masses and need to prepare a mass-matching reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-mass-matching-reference-building) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-mass-trace-detection**: Use when the input is centroided LC-MS data (mzML) and the goal is sensitive detection of mass traces (co-eluting signals of the same ion) as the precursor step to feature assembly and label-free metabolite quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-mass-trace-detection))
- **lc-ms-metabolic-feature-extraction**: Use when raw LC-MS mzML/mzXML files (Orbitrap or Q-TOF, MS1-only or DDA) must be turned into metabolic features plus pseudo MS/MS spectra before spectral library annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-metabolic-feature-extraction))
- **lc-ms-metabolic-feature-fidelity-assessment**: Use when a feature table contains suspect LC-MS features whose EIC shapes may be false positives, and you need automated good/poor peak-quality classification before downstream statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-metabolic-feature-fidelity-assessment))
- **lc-ms-metabolite-chromatogram-parsing**: Use when raw LC-MS MRM data are supplied as mzML files (e.g. from an Agilent 1290 Infinity II UHPLC coupled to an Agilent 6495 QQQ-MS) and chromatograms per metabolite, per sample must be extracted for preprocessing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-metabolite-chromatogram-parsing))
- **lc-ms-metabolomics-batch-correction**: Use when an unnormalized LC-MS metabolomics data matrix (rows = compounds, columns = samples) shows signal drift over acquisition order and batch effects that QC-only or non-robust normalization fails to fix, especially with outlier-contaminated features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-metabolomics-batch-correction))
- **lc-ms-metabolomics-data-normalization**: Use when an unnormalized LC-MS metabolomics matrix (compounds x runs) shows signal drift, batch effects, or occasional disproportionately high/low outlier intensities that deteriorate QC-based normalization, and you need batch-aware robust correction with QC-sample representativeness checking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-metabolomics-data-normalization))
- **lc-ms-metabolomics-data-processing**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS metabolomics data in .mzML (or .abf) format must be processed reproducibly via MS-DIAL and MS-FLO, and peak tables with retrospective provenance reports are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-metabolomics-data-processing))
- **lc-ms-metabolomics-peak-filtering**: Use when a detected chromatographic peak list from LC/MS metabolomics data must be quality-filtered before downstream statistical analysis, and true positives can be validated against a ground-truth peak list. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-metabolomics-peak-filtering))
- **lc-ms-metabolomics-training-data-preparation**: Use when building a deep-learning peak-quality classifier for LC-MS feature lists and you need labeled EIC plot images spanning diverse sample types, LC and MS conditions, with each plot manually inspected to assign good or poor quality labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-metabolomics-training-data-preparation))
- **lc-ms-mrm-preprocessing**: Use when raw MRM-mode LC-QQQ-MS data (e.g. mzML files from an Agilent 6495 QQQ-MS) must be processed into quality-scored chromatographic peak reports without manual peak review. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-mrm-preprocessing))
- **lc-ms-ms-feature-abundance-normalization**: Use when a margheRita mRList from MS-Dial LC-MS/MS profiling (e.g. the Urine dataset) has passed QC/filtering and sample abundances must be normalized, with PQN or log/reference methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-ms-feature-abundance-normalization))
- **lc-ms-ms-gc-ms-network-comparison**: Use when you have MCNs derived from multiple mass-spectrometry data types (e.g. LC-MS/MS and EI GC-MS) and need to assess whether network-level properties such as modularity or scale-free degree behaviour are consistent across platforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-ms-gc-ms-network-comparison))
- **lc-ms-ms-quality-control-job-configuration**: Use when Thermo Orbitrap .raw files are being written during an active LC-MS/MS run and you need near-real-time QC (instrument sensitivity, retention time shifts, mass accuracy) against a sequence .csv and library .msp. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-ms-quality-control-job-configuration))
- **lc-ms-ms2-feature-recognition**: Use when processing DDA LC-MS metabolomics mzXML files where conventional peak picking (XCMS-style) misses low-abundance or bad peak-shape features, and you want to quantify or recover those lost features via their MS2 spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-ms2-feature-recognition))
- **lc-ms-mz-to-pathway-mapping**: Use when LC-MS peak lists of m/z features (optionally with MS2-annotated compound names from pooled-QC spectra) need to be interpreted as pathway-level biology, e.g. identifying pathways changed between treatment groups in untargeted metabolomics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-mz-to-pathway-mapping))
- **lc-ms-noise-threshold-setting**: Use when processing LC-MS mzML files of authentic metabolite standards in metScribeR and a background noise level must be chosen from the noise plot before peak finding and filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-noise-threshold-setting))
- **lc-ms-noise-zigzag-assessment**: Use when processing LC/MS profile data (e.g. Agilent HPLC–Q-TOF Premier) where extracted ion chromatograms must be quality-scored and filtered prior to CWT-based chromatographic peak detection, or when detected peaks need to be ranked by sharpness, peak significance level, or zigzag index to. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-noise-zigzag-assessment))
- **lc-ms-output-validation**: Use when after executing a Nextflow-based LC-HRMS metabolomics workflow with Docker or Singularity containerization on .mzML LC-MS data, before proceeding to downstream statistical or visualization analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-output-validation) · 3 copies)
- **lc-ms-peak-annotation-counting**: Use when an LC-MS/MS untargeted metabolomics dataset (e.g., in vitro S9 incubation peak tables with MS2 spectra) has been annotated with KGMN and you must report per-mode known/unknown peak counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-annotation-counting))
- **lc-ms-peak-annotation-prioritization**: Use when untargeted LC-MS peak lists (mass-to-charge ratios with adducts/fragments) need to be annotated to KEGG compounds and a small prioritized proposal per peak is required, especially when standard annotation tools like xMSannotator underperform. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-annotation-prioritization))
- **lc-ms-peak-area-column-validation**: Use when importing tab-delimited peak-integrated metabolomics data into mzQuality and you need to verify the presence of mandatory columns (aliquot, compound, area, type, injection_time, batch) before downstream quality analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-area-column-validation))
- **lc-ms-peak-area-extraction**: Use when the analysis goal is to obtain quantitative peak-area values for specific metabolites (e.g. phenylacetyl-carnitine) from raw LC-MS/MS untargeted metabolomics files rather than relying on pre-computed feature tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-area-extraction))
- **lc-ms-peak-area-normalization**: Use when MultiQuant-exported peak areas and RTs from a targeted LC-MS/MS sequence show intensity drift and a compound needs IS correction, QC-based drift correction, custom bracketing, or weighted bracketing before external-calibration quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-area-normalization))
- **lc-ms-peak-detection-and-feature-alignment**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS chromatogram files (e.g., beer extracts in positive and negative ionization modes) must be converted into a bag-of-fragments matrix with MS2 features as rows and MS2 spectra as columns before LDA inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-detection-and-feature-alignment))
- **lc-ms-peak-detection-evaluation**: Use when evaluating or reproducing peak-detection accuracy on Orbitrap/QTOF LC-MS raw files against a manually labeled EIC ground-truth set, or comparing MassCube with MS-DIAL, MZmine3, and xcms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-detection-evaluation))
- **lc-ms-peak-feature-engineering**: Use when you have LC-MS feature tables with m/z and retention time coordinates paired with raw .mzXML or .mzML data files, and you need to systematically assess which features correspond to genuine chromatographic peaks versus noise or artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-peak-feature-engineering) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-peak-integration-quality-prediction**: Use when an XCMS-processed untargeted LC–MS feature table (e.g., from Metabolomics Workbench ST000726/ST000695-style data) contains poorly integrated peaks you need to flag automatically, and RSD &lt; 30% QC filtering alone is insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-integration-quality-prediction))
- **lc-ms-peak-picking-and-grouping**: Use when starting from raw mzML (or similar vendor) LC–MS files for untargeted metabolomics and a feature intensity table (metabolic features × samples) must be constructed before statistical analysis, annotation or QC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-picking-and-grouping))
- **lc-ms-peak-picking-bounding-box-extraction**: Use when you have full-scan mzML peak lists plus mzML files from DDA (TopN) controller injections and need per-sample coverage (peaks fragmented) and efficiency metrics for LC-MS/MS metabolomics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-picking-bounding-box-extraction))
- **lc-ms-peak-quality-classification**: Use when you have non-filtered MZmine 2 peak lists from nontargeted LC-MS metabolomics data and need to separate true spectral features from random false noise peaks prior to downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-quality-classification--151d13dd44185e00))
- **lc-ms-peak-quality-classification**: Use when an untargeted LC-MS metabolomics feature table produced by XCMS contains poorly integrated peaks that would propagate to downstream analyses, and automated Pass/Fail peak-quality classification is needed instead of manual expert assessment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-quality-classification--e4f52b320e986303))
- **lc-ms-peak-quality-filtering**: Use when processing raw mzML files of LC-MS metabolite standards to decide which adduct peaks (per EIC) are high-quality enough for RT/m/z library inclusion, and when benchmarking automated filtering against manual Good/Bad Peak labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-quality-filtering))
- **lc-ms-peak-quantification**: Use when after a CNN-Transformer peak detection model has been run on LC-MS ROI images and has output predicted peak locations with confidence scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-peak-quantification) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-peak-raster-image-generation**: Use when non-targeted LC-MS peak lists (e.g., MZmine 2 mzXML-derived features) must be transformed into per-peak raster images to feed an image-based CNN for true/false peak classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-raster-image-generation))
- **lc-ms-peak-screening-and-identification**: Use when performing semi-targeted LC-MS metabolomics analysis in MetaPro after peak integration, to apply quality-screening criteria and library-based peak identification using HMDB/METLIN-style spectral matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-screening-and-identification))
- **lc-ms-peak-shape-distortion-correction**: Use when ncGTW realignment has distorted peak shapes (broadened or narrowed peaks) in XCMS-flagged misaligned features, and corrected warping functions plus relocated peak positions are needed to return results to XCMS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-shape-distortion-correction))
- **lc-ms-peak-sharpness-and-significance-evaluation**: Use when you have candidate chromatographic peaks detected from LC/MS EICs (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-sharpness-and-significance-evaluation))
- **lc-ms-peak-table-quantification**: Use when raw LC-MS metabolomics data (.mzML or .abf) needs to be converted into a quantified peak table with peak detection, alignment, and filtering; especially when reproducibility across runs, containerization, or HPC execution is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-peak-table-quantification))
- **lc-ms-performance-quality-classification**: Use when evaluating whether DIA raw files (from Orbitrap, TripleTOF, or timsTOF series instruments) show degraded LC or MS performance, or when binary qualified/unqualified labels must be predicted from extracted QC metrics rather than inspected manually. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-performance-quality-classification))
- **lc-ms-pooled-qc-rsd-filtering**: Use when an LC–MS feature table contains pooled quality-control (QC) sample injections and noisy, low-reproducibility peaks must be removed with a simple, unsupervised reproducibility filter before statistical analysis or classifier training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-pooled-qc-rsd-filtering))
- **lc-ms-profile-data-segmentation**: Use when you have raw profile (not centroided) LC-MS data in .mzML format and need to prepare it for automated peak detection using a trained object detection network. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-profile-data-segmentation) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-qc-based-normalization**: Use when an unnormalized LC-MS metabolomics intensity matrix (e.g., runs x metabolites with QC annotations and batch labels) shows signal drift, batch effects, or outliers that degrade replicate concordance and downstream differential abundance testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-qc-based-normalization))
- **lc-ms-qc-consensus-labeling**: Use when multi-rater quality annotations of LC-MS files (e.g., qualified/unqualified per metric or per file) must be collapsed to a single consensus label and reproducibility (observed agreement) must be quantified per instrument. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-qc-consensus-labeling))
- **lc-ms-qc-rsd-calculation**: Use when a targeted LC-MS peak table (tab-delimited with aliquot, compound, area, type, injection_time, batch columns) contains pooled QC samples (SQC/LQC) and you need a per-compound QC-reproducibility metric for compound filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-qc-rsd-calculation))
- **lc-ms-qc-rule-implementation**: Use when a dashboard or QC pipeline needs to escalate per-internal-standard QC results into a specimen-level 'Warning' label using Rapid QC-MS's dropout-threshold and IS-Warn-fraction rule. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-qc-rule-implementation))
- **lc-ms-qc-sample-pca-visualization**: Use when a targeted LC-MS metabolomics study was measured in multiple batches and you need to verify, via PCA of sample types and batches, whether pooled QC (SQC) samples become aligned and centered across batches after batch correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-qc-sample-pca-visualization))
- **lc-ms-qualified-prediction**: Use when a DIA-MS raw file (.raw/.d/.wiff) from Orbitrap, TripleTOF, or timsTOF instruments must be assessed for LC/MS run quality and receive an automated qualified/unqualified prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-qualified-prediction))
- **lc-ms-quality-control-metric-extraction**: Use when Thermo .raw files are being acquired across a specimen sequence and per-specimen IS-derived RT, intensity, and Δm/z values must be tabulated to expose acute QC failures or gradual drift over hours, days, months, or years. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-quality-control-metric-extraction))
- **lc-ms-quality-metric-computation**: Use when after performing peak detection on centroided .mzML LC-MS data with screening_mode=FALSE in TARDIS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-quality-metric-computation) · 3 copies)
- **lc-ms-quantification-linear-quadratic-modeling**: Use when processing already-integrated targeted LC-MS/MS peak-area data (calibration standards plus samples) and you need per-compound automated calibration-curve regression without manual model/level tuning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-quantification-linear-quadratic-modeling))
- **lc-ms-raster-image-representation**: Use when raw LC-MS peak groups (e.g. MZmine 2 extracted features from nontargeted metabolomics runs) must be represented as images to train or apply a DNN-based true/false peak classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-raster-image-representation))
- **lc-ms-raw-data-import**: Use when a raw MS run (e.g. an mzML/TMT experiment such as PXD000001 acquired on a Thermo Orbitrap Q Exactive) must be loaded into R at scan level to plot retention-time vs m/z heat maps, 3D scan maps, or MS/MS2 spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/lc-ms-raw-data-import))
- **lc-ms-raw-data-peak-picking**: Use when raw LC-MS mzXML files (e.g., DDA data from an exposome or metabolomics mixture) need conventional XCMS peak picking as a feature table before JPA's MS2-recognition or targeted-list extraction steps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-raw-data-peak-picking))
- **lc-ms-raw-file-batch-processing**: Use when a study deposits or generates hundreds to thousands of vendor LC-MS raw files spanning multiple acquisition modes and instruments, and the goal is fully autonomous, mode-conditional identification without manual per-file intervention. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-raw-file-batch-processing))
- **lc-ms-raw-spectra-processing**: Use when starting from raw LC-MS1/MS2 spectral files (e.g. mzML/CDF vendor conversions of untargeted metabolomics or exposomics runs, including pooled-QC SWATH-DIA acquisitions) and you need feature detection, quantification, and MS2 deconvolution before annotation or statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-raw-spectra-processing))
- **lc-ms-reference-metabolite-validation**: Use when a raw mzXML/mzML standard-mixture file and its extracted library table must be screened so that only metabolite peaks passing Std/Blk ratio, RT RSD, and intensity thresholds enter the validated reference library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-reference-metabolite-validation))
- **lc-ms-retention-time-adjustment**: Use when you have centroided .mzML LC–MS data with multiple sample runs and a preliminary compound target table with theoretical or measured retention times, but you suspect the expected RT values may not align with actual retention windows in your dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-retention-time-adjustment) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-retention-time-alignment**: Use when XCMS-class single-warping-function alignment has produced misaligned feature groups in LC-MS profiles — detected by ncGTW's p-value-based misalignment detection — and the goal is compound-specific realignment before re-grouping and peak-filling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-retention-time-alignment--a3d0c74081c16ec0))
- **lc-ms-retention-time-alignment**: Use when aligned LC-MS feature tables across many runs show retention time drift (e.g., tens of seconds in RPLC negative mode) and features must be mapped to a common RT scale before statistical analysis or phenotype prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-retention-time-alignment--417be7244a00cf55))
- **lc-ms-retention-time-alignment**: Use when you have per-run feature lists (m/z, charge, RT, intensity) from Dinosaur, OpenMS, MaxQuant, XICFinder or TXT/CSV, need to align corresponding features/peptides across many LC-MS runs, or compare against MBR-based tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-retention-time-alignment--8a881f723a21b589))
- **lc-ms-retention-time-benchmarking**: Use when you have metabolite standard peak lists from both a manual vendor tool (e.g. MassHunter) and a semiautomated RT assignment pipeline (e.g. metScribeR) and need to quantify RT assignment accuracy for library quality control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-retention-time-benchmarking))
- **lc-ms-retention-time-drift-correction**: Use when aligning peak lists from multiple LC-MS samples whose retention times drift nonlinearly, and a naive join-by-m/z/RT tolerance fails; requires per-sample peak lists plus m/z and RT thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-retention-time-drift-correction))
- **lc-ms-retention-time-precision-analysis**: Use when replicate LC-MS runs of chemical standards are available (e.g. each standard mixture evaluated three times) and you need to quantify retention time reproducibility or filter peaks by an RSD threshold before accepting a metabolite as validated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-retention-time-precision-analysis))
- **lc-ms-retention-time-tracking**: Use when a longitudinal series of DIA raw files (.wiff/.raw/.d) from a fixed instrument must be quantified consistently and per-precursor retention-time/intensity stability (CV) needs to be summarized over time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-retention-time-tracking))
- **lc-ms-roi-annotation-interpretation**: Use when when you have extracted LC-MS ROI windows (m/z × retention time snippets) from raw mzML data and need to distinguish genuine metabolite peaks from noise or artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-roi-annotation-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **lc-ms-roi-detection**: Use when raw LC/MS mzML files must be mined for peaks without a pre-existing peak list — e.g. recovering signals an XCMS run missed, or benchmarking how many unique (non-degenerate) peaks a deep-learning detector adds over a conventional peak-picker. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-roi-detection))
- **lc-ms-roi-signal-segmentation**: Use when raw LC-MS mzML or Bruker .d files contain noisy MS1 mass traces that must be cut into chromatographic peak segments without biasing final peak parameters, or when a peak detector must achieve complete signal coverage over each ROI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-roi-signal-segmentation))
- **lc-ms-rt-mz-heatmap-visualization**: Use when raw LC-MS data (.npy/.mzML) has been classified by a trained DeepMSProfiler ensemble and you need per-class importance heatmaps on the 341×341 RT×m/z grid, e.g. for unknown-metabolite biomarker inspection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-rt-mz-heatmap-visualization))
- **lc-ms-run-simulation**: Use when you need gold-standard synthetic mzML datasets with known ground truth — e.g. to benchmark quantification algorithms, test mass-accuracy (ppm) sensitivity, or predict co-elution/co-fragmentation before real MS experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-run-simulation))
- **lc-ms-scan-acquisition-orchestration**: Use when when you have a curated list of chemical compounds (real or virtual), a defined fragmentation strategy (e.g., Top-N, exclusion lists), and need to simulate how that strategy will acquire MS1 and MS2 scans over a defined retention-time window. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-scan-acquisition-orchestration) · 3 copies)
- **lc-ms-semi-targeted-peak-detection**: Use when the goal is to reproduce automated semi-targeted quantification of predefined analytes and internal standards from vendor DDA LC-MS files (Thermo .raw, SCIEX .wiff) without a spectral library or manual curation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-semi-targeted-peak-detection))
- **lc-ms-serum-metabolomics-classification**: Use when the task is to classify raw untargeted LC-MS serum samples (mzML or .npy) into tri-class disease labels — healthy / lung nodule / lung cancer — or to reproduce the 0.961 early-stage lung adenocarcinoma accuracy via a pre-trained ensemble model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-serum-metabolomics-classification))
- **lc-ms-signal-drift-correction**: Use when a preprocessed LC-MS peak table (e.g. MS-DIAL Excel export) contains pooled QC samples measured across the run and features show systematic intensity drift with injection order that must be corrected before statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-signal-drift-correction))
- **lc-ms-signal-drift-evaluation**: Use when untargeted LC–MS data in mzML format contains pooled/intrastudy QC injections acquired over time and you need to detect, visualize, or correct time-dependent signal drift before downstream statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-signal-drift-evaluation))
- **lc-ms-simulated-data-metrics**: Use when evaluating a feature-matching workflow on synthetic LC-MS metabolomics data where the true correspondences between reference and target feature tables are known by construction, and a match-correctness fraction must be reported. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-simulated-data-metrics))
- **lc-ms-spectra-file-handling**: Use when loading untargeted LC-MS metabolomics data (feature table plus MS2 fragmentation spectra) into an annotation pipeline and deciding between .mgf and .mzML spectral file formats. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-spectra-file-handling))
- **lc-ms-spectral-data-import**: Use when you have raw LC-MS/MS spectral data in .mgf format (or vendor-specific raw data that can be converted to .mgf via MZmine or similar tools) and need to prepare it for interactive exploration using the specXplore dashboard. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lc-ms-spectral-data-import) · 3 copies)
- **lc-ms-spike-in-reference-matching**: Use when you have repeated spike-in LC-MS measurements (e.g. human plasma background) plus a ground-truth list of reference analytes, and need to quantify detection sensitivity/specificity or verify the &gt;95% claim. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-spike-in-reference-matching))
- **lc-ms-system-suitability-testing**: Use when LC-HRMS acquisitions (mzML of a spiked SST standard mixture) must be checked for system suitability — retention-time stability, EIC peak areas, or MS/MS product-ion/neutral-loss behavior — before collecting study data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-system-suitability-testing))
- **lc-ms-targeted-metabolomics-qc-outlier-detection**: Use when evaluating targeted LC-MS metabolomics peak tables (tab-delimited with aliquot, compound, area, type, injection_time, batch columns) to identify mis-injections and pooled-QC outliers before batch correction or statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-targeted-metabolomics-qc-outlier-detection))
- **lc-ms-time-series-feature-extraction**: Use when a time-course LC-MS peak table (sample rows, feature columns, time-point labels such as T0–T48) has been scanned by NOREVA's workflow ranking and you must extract a known rhythmic metabolite's trace from top- and bottom-ranked workflow outputs to visually validate processing quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-time-series-feature-extraction))
- **lc-ms-vendor-software-benchmarking**: Use when peak areas from an automated integrator must be validated against an experienced analyst's vendor-software (e.g., Xcalibur) peak areas using correlation, regression, and reproducibility metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-vendor-software-benchmarking))
- **lc-ms-vs-afadesi-msi-adduct-difference-evaluation**: Use when building or extending a metabolite annotation database for AFADESI-MSI data: metabolites identified by LC-MS/MS in tissue extracts must be re-represented as the adduct ions actually formed during ambient MSI desorption before m/z matching can be done. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-vs-afadesi-msi-adduct-difference-evaluation))
- **lc-ms-workflow-execution**: Use when raw LC-MS data (.mzML or .abf) must be processed reproducibly end-to-end — peak detection through filtering, alignment, and contaminant removal — on a Unix-like machine, container, or HPC/SLURM cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms-workflow-execution))
- **lc-ms1-feature-detection-and-quantification**: Use when starting from raw, centroided LC-MS1 spectra (e.g. mzML files) and you need peak picking, peak alignment, gap filling, and peak annotation to produce a full MS1 feature table for quantification or dilution-series correlation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms1-feature-detection-and-quantification))
- **lc-ms2-feature-annotation**: Use when a crude natural product or metabolomics extract has been acquired by LC-MS2 and you need to prioritize which molecular features to fractionate or isolate based on spectral similarity and dereplication. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms2-feature-annotation))
- **lc-ms2-fragment-matching**: Use when LC-MS2 data from fractionated natural-product extracts must be dereplicated against a reference compound (e.g. swinholide A) to prioritize fractions and annotate known or structural-hybrid macrolides. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms2-fragment-matching))
- **lc-ms2-molecular-networking-interpretation**: Use when LC-MS/MS data from fractionated crude natural-product extracts (e.g. marine cyanobacterial Symploca fractions) must be clustered by MS2 similarity to prioritize which fractions to isolate and annotate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-ms2-molecular-networking-interpretation))
- **lc-msms-annotation-accuracy-assessment**: Use when a processed LC-MS/MS feature table with SIRIUS/CSI:FingerID annotations must be checked against known expected compounds (e.g. actinomycete secondary metabolites or commercial standards) to report detection and annotation accuracy fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-annotation-accuracy-assessment))
- **lc-msms-annotation-yield-comparison**: Use when comparing annotation coverage of unsupervised motif-based substructure discovery (MS2LDA Mass2Motifs) against spectral library matching (Mass Bank/NIST) on the same MS/MS spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-annotation-yield-comparison))
- **lc-msms-biomarker-list-filtering**: Use when MALDI imaging proteolytic peaks (m/z [M+H]+ list) need to be annotated against LC-MS/MS proteomic discovery data, and a gene-name cluster list must first be used to restrict candidate biomarkers before correlation matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/lc-msms-biomarker-list-filtering))
- **lc-msms-chromatogram-simulation**: Use when the goal is to generate a gold-standard synthetic mzML for LC-MS/MS (peptides, nucleosides, or lipids defined by chemical formulas) and benchmark it against a real run, e.g. reproducing a 140-min proteomics gradient simulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-chromatogram-simulation))
- **lc-msms-consensus-spectra-processing**: Use when a multi-sample LC-MS/MS untargeted metabolomics data set (e.g. MSV000080502 Euphorbia dendroides extract) must be turned into clean consensus spectra with ionization variants (in-source fragments, adducts, dimers) merged into one representative [M+H]+ ion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-consensus-spectra-processing))
- **lc-msms-dda-run-simulation**: Use when a gold-standard LC-MS/MS dataset with known ground truth is needed to benchmark peptide identification pipelines, test search/rescoring algorithms, or predict co-elution and co-fragmentation before running real MS experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-dda-run-simulation))
- **lc-msms-feature-alignment**: Use when aligning per-sample precursor (MS1) and fragment (MS2) m/z/intensity feature lists exported from MS-DIAL across many LC-MS/MS samples into one feature table for downstream multivariate or machine-learning analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-alignment))
- **lc-msms-feature-annotation-msi-level3**: Use when mzML files and OpenMS-annotated featureXML adduct information are available and you need to attach SIRIUS molecular formula and CSI:FingerID structural identifications (MSI level 3) to detected LC-MS/MS features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-annotation-msi-level3))
- **lc-msms-feature-based-molecular-networking-processing**: Use when the analysis goal is to detect, align, quantify and dereplicate LC-MS/MS features from natural product extract fractions in order to generate an MS/MS-annotated feature table for downstream bioactivity-correlation mapping (e.g., antiviral screening). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-based-molecular-networking-processing))
- **lc-msms-feature-count-validation**: Use when running the specXplore importing pipeline on .mgf MS/MS feature lists (e.g. wheat or urine exposome data) and you need a scale sanity check — wall-clock preprocessing time and post-processing feature count — before opening the dashboard session. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-count-validation))
- **lc-msms-feature-detection**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS runs in .mzML or .mzXML format from untargeted metabolomics experiments must be converted into features (precursor ion, adduct, isotope peaks) with merged fragmentation spectra per feature before de novo formula and structure annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-detection))
- **lc-msms-feature-detection-and-alignment**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS files from bioassay-guided fractionation must be converted into a feature-level table aligned across fractions before Pearson correlation scoring and GNPS FBMN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-detection-and-alignment))
